Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the parsing framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 561. The resulting operator achieves bound O(538/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the parsing decomposition. By minimality, the element a^561 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 538-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
